# KTM 125 XC 2024: aftermarket parts

4676 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 431 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 1680 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 2565 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
